## Round 8

- **Board judged**: 463fb5a91cc9c25e91dece221c3f6541ad1a16a0 (HEAD of the wall board branch, read in a detached worktree outside the repository). The design files are those of `56660c8021`, "critical routing pass 4" (`git diff --name-only 9e6bb8ee 463fb5a9` lists the records `design-rules.md`, `fabrication-notes.md`, `layout-review.md`, `owner-sign-off.md`, `sweep-log.md` and one KiCad file, `heater-wall-board.kicad_pcb`; no sheet, no project file `heater-wall-board.kicad_pro`, nothing in `heater-wall-board.pretty`, no drill, outline, pad or track differs). Against the board round 7 judged (`9e6bb8ee`, end-of-round commit `921b5eb9`) `git diff 9e6bb8ee 463fb5a9` of `heater-wall-board.kicad_pcb` was read in full here (178 changed lines) and holds only these blocks: (a) the front OUT_C zone `OUT_C_F0` (locked): outline vertices `(56.45, 25.4) (53.4, 28.45) (53.4, 35.9) (49.3, 40) (49.3, 46.425) (58.1, 46.425) (58.1, 36.2) (58.4, 35.9) (58.4, 25.85) (57.95, 25.4)` become `(56.45, 24.97) (52.95, 28.47) (52.95, 36.35) (49.3, 40) (49.3, 46.425) (57.95, 46.425)` with the right edge one line at x 57.95 up to the neck, and the filled polygon refilled to match (63 vertices); (b) the six blade legend texts `RET`, `RETURN`, `OUT_S`, `STEAM`, `OUT_C` and `COFFEE` turned to 90 degrees at (23.475, 79.9), (25.6, 79.9), (31.575, 79.9), (33.7, 79.9), (38.45, 82.0) and (40.575, 82.0); (c) the RV921 reference property from `(at 2.5 -2.8 0)` to `(at 2.35 6.8 90)`, which places it at (28.65, 35.7) reading upward; (d) the maker block, the logo's graphics and the two texts `MADE WITH` and `STAIRCASE`, moved +0.5 mm in x (the logo now spans x 47.05 to 50.1, the texts start at x 50.65). Nothing else differs.
- **Views**: this round did not run `task hw:views` or `task hw:placement` (disk free about 7 GB; the brief asked for tiles at 40 to 50 pixels per millimetre and closer), so the index, the net views and the placement view of the method are not in this round; every verdict rests on views rendered here from the detached worktree. Commands (KiCad 10.0.7, rsvg-convert, `KICAD_CLI=/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/MacOS/kicad-cli`):

```
kicad-cli pcb export svg --layers F.Cu,F.SilkS,Edge.Cuts --mode-single --exclude-drawing-sheet --page-size-mode 2 -o fc.svg heater-wall-board.kicad_pcb
kicad-cli pcb export svg --layers B.Cu,Edge.Cuts --mode-single --exclude-drawing-sheet --page-size-mode 2 -o bc.svg heater-wall-board.kicad_pcb
kicad-cli pcb export svg --layers F.SilkS,Edge.Cuts --mode-single --exclude-drawing-sheet --page-size-mode 2 -o fs.svg heater-wall-board.kicad_pcb
kicad-cli pcb export svg --layers F.Cu,Edge.Cuts ... -o cuonly.svg ; kicad-cli pcb export svg --layers F.SilkS,Edge.Cuts ... -o silkonly.svg
rsvg-convert -w 2024 -b "#101010" fc.svg -o fc.png (the same for bc and fs: 45 pixels per millimetre, board x 20 to 65, y 21 to 96)
rsvg-convert -w 4498 fc.svg -o fc100.png (100 pixels per millimetre, crops z_wedge, z_leg, z_maker, z_via, z_t911, z_t921)
rsvg-convert on the svg with its viewBox cut to a window at 200 pixels per millimetre (k1, k2)
rsvg-convert -w 2998 cuonly.svg and silkonly.svg (66.6 pixels per millimetre, the silk-over-copper test ov_silk)
```

  **Tiles looked at, every one, as images at 45 pixels per millimetre.** The grid is 4 columns by 7 rows, 12.05 mm by 11.5 mm; column c0 is x 19.6 to 31.65, c1 x 30.85 to 42.9, c2 x 42.1 to 54.15, c3 x 53.35 to 65.4; row r0 is y 20.6 to 32.1, r1 y 31.3 to 42.8, r2 y 42.0 to 53.5, r3 y 52.7 to 64.2, r4 y 63.5 to 75.0, r5 y 74.2 to 85.7, r6 y 84.9 to 96.4. Each tile shows three panels at the same coordinates: F.Cu with F.SilkS over it, B.Cu, and F.SilkS alone. The 28 tiles looked at: T00 T01 T02 T03, T10 T11 T12 T13, T20 T21 T22 T23, T30 T31 T32 T33, T40 T41 T42 T43, T50 T51 T52 T53, T60 T61 T62 T63. Also looked at as images: `z_wedge` (x 47 to 61, y 20.5 to 48, F.Cu with silk, 94 px/mm as displayed: the new OUT_C shape), `z_leg` (x 21 to 49, y 76 to 92, 91 px/mm: the six legends, the J-references and J911's copper), `z_maker` (x 44 to 65, y 84 to 96, 100 px/mm: the moved maker block, the title block, H1), `z_chains` (z_t921 beside z_t911, x 29 to 39 and 51 to 61, y 21.5 to 31.5, 70 px/mm: the two identical chains), `z_via` (x 55.5 to 65, y 53.5 to 63, 70 px/mm: the via, J931, the U921 pads), `k1` (x 52.5 to 59, y 22 to 30, 200 px/mm: the new left chamfer meeting the neck) and `k2` (x 47.5 to 54, y 33.5 to 41.5, 200 px/mm: the lower left chamfer and RV911 pad 1). Not looked at as images, and not rendered: B.Cu at more than 45 px/mm (B.Cu has no change since round 7 and was looked at on all 28 tiles), F.Mask, B.Mask, F.Paste, B.Paste, F.Fab, B.Fab, F.CrtYd, B.CrtYd, the per-net views, the placement view, the assembly and 3D views and the hw:views index. The silk-over-copper test `ov_silk` was a computed test, not an image: every connected silk shape (271) was tested against the copper raster of its own face. Computed from the board file, not seen: the interior angle of every vertex of the 14 zones' outlines and filled polygons, the 15 references' positions, the 25 gr_text, the 80 silk graphics' widths, the 20 tracks' lengths and angles, the 22 locked items.

### Plan and free pass

**Plan against layout.** The floorplan holds: the six rule areas stand at the plan's bounds on both copper layers (read from the board file's zone polygons: mains (20, 20) to (65, 50.19), (20, 50.19) to (47, 66.21), (20, 66.21) to (65, 88), (20, 88) to (55, 96); low voltage (56, 50.19) to (65, 66.21) and (55, 88) to (65, 96)); the connector table holds (J941, J921 and J911 at x 24.50, 32.60 and 44.00, y 90.04; J931 pin 1 at (63.18, 58.20)); the outline is (20.0, 21.0) to (65.0, 96.0), 45.0 by 75.0 mm, with the two 1.5 mm slots open at the right edge; H1 at (61.50, 92.50); all 15 footprints on the front, none on the back. The plan's `yes` rows and the board's own items were read against the tiles and the board file, each by its verdict row. The plan's `MFG-11` row says the blade legends stand "above J941, J921 and J911" at the positions `fabrication-notes.md` states; the layout keeps them north of the blades at those positions, with the `OUT_C` pair beside J911's copper on laminate, so no departure. The plan rows that name a spur at U921 pin 4, a via on the OUT_S spur and a `HEAT_S_OPTO_MT` run to pin 6 are baseline rows written before placement, carried by the amendment notes and the later passes; no departure is new in this round.

**What changed since round 7, read from the files.** Only the four blocks the opening lines list. Judged on the board as it stands:

- The front OUT_C zone. Outline vertices: the neck at the pad 2 (the right edge x 57.95, straight, 22.67 mm from (57.95, 23.76) to (57.95, 46.425), with no step and no chamfer), the left edge leaving the neck as a 45-degree run from (56.45, 24.97) to (52.95, 28.47), straight down x 52.95 for 7.88 mm to (52.95, 36.35), a 45-degree run of 5.16 mm to (49.3, 40), straight down x 49.3 to (49.3, 46.425), and along y 46.425 to (57.95, 46.425). My own computation of the interior angle at each vertex gives 225, 135, 225, 135, 90 and 90 degrees at the six corners and nothing under 90 degrees in the outline or in the filled polygon; there is no notch, no sliver and no step left. The strip is 5.0 mm wide (x 52.95 to 57.95), the same as OUT_S's strip (x 30.0 to 35.0), and its right edge is flush with the right edge of T911 pad 2 (57.2 plus 0.75 mm). The body below is 8.65 mm wide. U911 pad 6 projects 0.8 mm past the body's right edge at y 44.43 to 46.43, which is the pad's own outline. The vertical edge x 49.3 is tangent to RV911 pad 1's left edge. All of it stays 1.5 mm or more from every other net: the DRC reports only the accepted 1.04 mm pad pairs, and I measured the filled chamfer's end of straight run against T911 pin 1 at 1.65 mm. At `k1` (200 px/mm) the 45-degree chamfer meets the clearance arc round T911 pin 1 at a corner of about 15 degrees (the chamfer line x plus y equal to 81.42 passes 0.02 mm inside the arc's circle, so the straight run ends where the arc begins and the two meet at a small bend); the OUT_S neck at `z_chains` has a square shoulder and then a chamfer instead. Seen, not raised: both are the zone fill's own clearance arc at a pad neck, the waist and chamfers into the pads are accepted shapes, the bend is visible only above about 150 px/mm and nothing about it affects function, creepage or clearance.
- The legends. `RET` (1.5 mm) at (23.475, 79.9) and `RETURN` (1.0 mm) at (25.6, 79.9) stand wholly inside the RET strip (x 21.5 to 27.3, 1.1 mm clear of each strip edge); `OUT_S` (1.5 mm) at (31.575, 79.9) and `STEAM` (1.0 mm) at (33.7, 79.9) wholly inside the OUT_S strip (x 30.0 to 35.0, 0.6 and 0.7 mm clear); `OUT_C` (1.5 mm) at (38.45, 82.0) and `COFFEE` (1.0 mm) at (40.575, 82.0) wholly on laminate (x 37.7 to 41.1), 0.45 mm left of the J911 copper (x 41.55) and 0.5 mm below the OUT_S track's lower edge (y 78.25). All six read upward, every pair is centred on its anchors, and the first two pairs share the centre line y 79.9. The OUT_C pair's centre is 2.1 mm lower (y 82.0), since centred on y 79.9 its 6.7 mm of text would touch the OUT_S track at y 76.15 to 78.25 (`z_leg`); the J941, J921 and J911 references stand level at y 84.2. Judged: wholly on copper or wholly on laminate, upright to the bottom-to-top reading rule, aligned by pair; the OUT_C pair's lower centre is a consequence of the track and is not raised. The silk-over-copper test (ov_silk) found no legend, reference or text across a copper edge.

At (28.65, 35.7) reading upward, in the 2.7 mm laminate gap between the RET strip (edge x 27.3) and the OUT_S strip (edge x 30.0), x 28.15 to 29.15 (0.85 mm clear each side), above its body (`T10`): wholly on laminate, no longer crossing the strip edges. **Dead copper, necks, asymmetry, tested.** Every zone has one filled polygon per layer and no island (read from the file); every track ends on a pad, a via, a zone or another track of its net (KiCad's `track_dangling` is clean); no zone has copper behind a pad that nothing joins; the front OUT_C zone's vertex at (49.3, 40) stops at RV911 pad 1's left tangent with no copper behind the pad. The necks are the two triac pin-2 waists of 0.58 mm (accepted, not raised), the 0.98 mm finger of OUT_S that carries only the opto-triac's gate current (OUT_S_F0 x 62.95 to 63.93, y 70.0 to 77.2), the B.Cu tab of RET at RV911 (3.5 mm) and the chamfers into the pads; none is new. The two chains are identical in their parts and differ in copper as the circuit requires: T911 and T921 stand at the same pad row (y 22.86); the gate tracks leave pin 3 at 45 degrees to a 0.5 mm column (x 62.0 and x 38.6); R912 and R922 are the same resistor turned the same way with their references on the side away from the strips; the references of T911 and T921 stand mirrored (6.06 mm left of T911's origin and 11.14 mm right of T921's, y 22.7); the front OUT_C zone's strip is now 5.0 mm like OUT_S's, its right edge flush with pad 2's right edge, its left edge a chamfer and a vertical where OUT_S's front zone is symmetric about its pin: that is the plan's routing (BOARD-4), not a leftover.

### Plan and free pass

**Plan against layout.** The floorplan holds: the six rule areas stand at the plan's bounds on both copper layers, read from the board file's zone polygons and drawn on every placement tile (mains (20, 20) to (65, 50.19), (20, 50.19) to (47, 66.21), (20, 66.21) to (65, 88), (20, 88) to (55, 96); low voltage (56, 50.19) to (65, 66.21) and (55, 88) to (65, 96)); the connector table holds (J941, J921 and J911 at x 24.50, 32.60 and 44.00, y 90.04; J931 pin 1 at (63.18, 58.20)); the outline is (20.0, 21.0) to (65.0, 96.0), 45.0 by 75.0 mm, with the two 1.5 mm slots (51.0, 49.44)-(65.0, 50.94) and (51.0, 65.46)-(65.0, 66.96) open at the right edge, round-ended at x 51.0, read from the Edge.Cuts lines and arcs; H1 stands at (61.50, 92.50); all 15 footprints are on the front, none on the back; the placement command measured 0 parts outside their regions. The plan's `yes` rows and the board's own items were read against the views and the board file, each by its verdict row. The departures I found are the two record statements of finding 9.1 and finding 9.2; the board itself departs from no plan row that an earlier closure does not already cover. Where the plan's baseline BOARD-8 row and relaxation R3 quote 20.73 mm and 20.7 mm for U911, the board's figures are 22.41 mm body to body, 19.59 mm courtyard to body and 19.82 mm nearest pad to body (finding 9.2).

**What changed since round 8, read from the files.** No design file: the board, the project file, the rules file, the sheets and the library are as round 8 judged them. The three records changed after round 8 were read in full and checked against the board: `production-files-check.md` (the production-files record at `478d3b05`: the placement rows, 8 rows for the 8 placed through-hole parts and the six hand-fitted parts listed only under the parts list; the layer verdicts; the drill count of 21 holes, 0.3 mm x1, 0.6 mm x4, 1.0 mm x3, 1.1 mm x6, 1.4 mm x6 and the 3.2 mm hole, which is the board's 20 drilled pads and one via; the figures against the maker's limits) agrees with the board file: pad and hole figures reproduced (annular ring (1.5 minus 1.1) / 2 = 0.20 mm at the triacs, 2.54 minus 1.1 = 1.44 mm hole to hole, the via 0.90 mm from J931 pin 3's hole, J931 pin 1 pad 0.97 mm from the right edge at x 65.0 less 64.03), and its 12 DRC findings equal mine; `fabrication-notes.md`, "Fitting order of the triacs and the blades", states the tab hole at 17.25 mm (layout-hedges.md hedges 8 and 16), the order of tray, bolting the tab to the plate and then soldering the leads, and the blades last with the board supported (hedge 9), and is consistent with the "Home application steps" paragraph, which fits every hand-fitted part before the coat; `sweep-log.md` records the sweep of that class (phrases and scope given; I re-ran the same phrases and found the same places, `controller-board-division.md` carrying the earlier bracket form's order with its own amendment note).

## Round 10

- **Reviewer**: the reviewer is a fresh-context agent that did not draw, edit, place or route this board and has no memory of earlier rounds. It read the board's design files, the method, the hardware readme, the committed `layout-plan.md`, the records of the board's folder and the rendered views; rounds 1 to 9 of this record were read as history, never as evidence, and every verdict below was judged on the board and the records as this commit holds them.
- **Views**: every view the method's layout review views section asks for was rendered with the two commands below, and these were opened and looked at as images by the reviewer: all 77 images of `hw:views` and all 4 of `hw:placement`, and, further, 24 sheets of my own zoom tiles, each sheet four tiles of 45 px/mm set two by two at their own scale, 1012 by 964 pixels, holding the 84 zoom tiles (28 for each of F.Cu, B.Cu and F.SilkS; the lower half of each sheet of rows 7 and 8 is empty because the grid has seven rows), every one looked at. From `hw:views` (resolution 30 px/mm, each image within the 1568 pixel limit, tiles named `-r<row>c1`): the 38 layer tiles `layer-F.Cu` and `layer-B.Cu` (r1 to r4 each), `layer-F.SilkS`, `layer-B.SilkS`, `layer-Edge.Cuts`, `layer-F.Mask`, `layer-B.Mask`, `layer-F.Paste`, `layer-B.Paste`, `layer-F.Fab`, `layer-F.CrtYd` and `layer-Cmts.User` (r1 to r3 each); `classes-r1c1` to `classes-r4c1`; `assembly-top-r1c1` to `-r4c1` and `assembly-bottom-r1c1` to `-r4c1`; `crop-U911` and `crop-U921`; `3d-top`, `3d-bottom` and `3d-angled`; the 22 written net tiles (`net-_RET` r1 to r4, `net-_Steam_heater_switch_OUT_S` r1 to r4, `net-_Coffee_heater_switch_OUT_C` r1 to r4, `net-_Steam_heater_switch_HEAT_S_GATE_R` r1 to r3, `net-_Coffee_heater_switch_HEAT_C_GATE_R-r1c1`, `net-_Coffee_heater_switch_HEAT_C_OPTO_MT-r2c1`, `net-_Coffee_heater_switch_HEAT_C_TRIAC_G-r1c1`, `net-_Steam_heater_switch_HEAT_S_OPTO_MT-r3c1`, `net-_Steam_heater_switch_HEAT_S_TRIAC_G-r1c1`, `net-unconnected-_U911-NC-Pad5-r2c1`, `net-unconnected-_U921-NC-Pad5-r3c1`), the tiles holding none of a net's copper not being written, as each net's `NET TILES` line says. Not opened: `index.json`, `facts.json` and `measures.json`, the machine twins of those texts. Not rendered: B.Fab, B.CrtYd and the other layers on which the board file holds no item (its items lie on B.Cu, B.SilkS, Cmts.User, Edge.Cuts, F.CrtYd, F.Cu, F.Fab and F.SilkS, and the pad stacks give the mask and paste layers), which the views command writes no image for; the 3D views draw no part models, as their captions say (every footprint declares none), so the assembled look of the parts was read from the assembly views and the F.Fab and F.CrtYd layers.
- **Further views**: the zoom tiles are named `zoom-<layer>-r<row>c<col>` with row 1 to 7 and column 1 to 4, each tile 506 by 482 pixels at 45 px/mm, about 11.3 by 10.7 mm, covering board x 20.0 to 65.0 and y 21.0 to 96.0, labelled with its board coordinates. They were drawn from KiCad's own SVG export, then rasterised and cut (`<board>` is heater-wall-board.kicad_pcb, run in the folder of the board):

```
kicad-cli pcb export svg --layers F.Cu,Edge.Cuts --mode-single --exclude-drawing-sheet --page-size-mode 2 -o fc.svg heater-wall-board.kicad_pcb
kicad-cli pcb export svg --layers B.Cu,Edge.Cuts --mode-single --exclude-drawing-sheet --page-size-mode 2 -o bc.svg heater-wall-board.kicad_pcb
kicad-cli pcb export svg --layers F.SilkS,Edge.Cuts --mode-single --exclude-drawing-sheet --page-size-mode 2 -o fs.svg heater-wall-board.kicad_pcb
rsvg-convert -w 2024 -b "#101010" fc.svg -o fc.png (the same for bc and fs; 44.99 px/mm; 28 tiles cut from each by a script of a few lines)
```

### Plan and free pass

**Plan against layout.** The floorplan holds: the six rule areas stand at the plan's bounds on both copper layers, read from the board file's zone polygons and drawn on every placement tile (mains (20, 20) to (65, 50.19), (20, 50.19) to (47, 66.21), (20, 66.21) to (65, 88), (20, 88) to (55, 96); low voltage (56, 50.19) to (65, 66.21) and (55, 88) to (65, 96)); the connector table holds (J941, J921 and J911 at x 24.50, 32.60 and 44.00, y 90.04; J931 pin 1 at (63.18, 58.20), pins 2 and 3 at 60.64 and 58.10); the outline is (20.0, 21.0) to (65.0, 96.0), 45.0 by 75.0 mm, with the two 1.5 mm slots (51.0, 49.44)-(65.0, 50.94) and (51.0, 65.46)-(65.0, 66.96) open at the right edge, round-ended at x 51.0, read from the Edge.Cuts lines and arcs; H1 stands at (61.50, 92.50); all 15 footprints are on the front, none on the back; the placement command measured 0 parts outside their regions. The plan's `yes` rows and the board's own items were read against the views and the board file, each by its verdict row, and the board departs from no row that an earlier closure does not already cover. Where the baseline BOARD-8 row and relaxation R3 quote 20.73 mm and 20.7 mm for U911, the board's figures are 22.41 mm body to body, 19.59 mm courtyard to body and 19.82 mm nearest pad to body, which `layout-plan.md` line 16 and its amendment note after round 9 state and item 9 of the owner's end-of-work list carries; I reproduced them from the board file (U911's F.Fab outline top at y 47.0 and its courtyard top at y 44.19 against the T911 body front at y 24.6, and its pad at y 44.425).

**What changed since round 9, read from the files.** No design file. The one commit after `aea833b7`, `026c8053`, changed `owner-sign-off.md` and `sweep-log.md` only; I read both changes in full: the four closing-round statements of `owner-sign-off.md` (lines 23, 96 and 109, and the first-look list's W-13 line) now name the last round recorded in `layout-review.md` and no round number, the Round 5 mentions in W-13 being the owner's words' history, and `sweep-log.md` holds the matching dated section; item 9 (BOARD-8 on body distance) and the 22.41, 19.59 and 19.82 mm figures stand as round 9 left them and agree with the board. The staging folder `docs/staging-from-wall-board-session/` (README, four subfolders of scripts and notes) states that nothing in it is part of the design record and nothing there is run by a test or the gate; no record of the board's folder names it, and nothing in it states a figure of this board that the board or its records contradict. The graph evidence node of the layout review's criterion (read with `staircase show`, status superseded) speaks of rounds 1 to 5 and is history, not a live statement of the board folder.

ACCEPTED | hardware/sunbeam-em7000/heater-wall-board/heater-wall-board.kicad_pro | spacing_exception | hardware/sunbeam-em7000/heater-wall-board/separation-basis.yaml accepted_exceptions[0] | accepted exception: mains_to_mains creepage between R911.1 and R911.2 (Pad 2 [/Coffee heater switch/HEAT_C_GATE_R] of R911 on F.Cu; Pad 1 [/Coffee heater switch/HEAT_C_OPTO_MT] of R911 on F.Cu) measures 1.8 mm, accepted down to 1.79 mm against the signed 2.6 mm; reason: R911 (200 ohm, R_1206_3216Metric, UNI-ROYAL C18002) is one of the two series gate resistors of its heater's gate network (R911 and R912, the coffee side), between the opto-triac's output and the triac gate. Its two pads stand 1.80 mm apart edge to edge, which is the 1206 package's own pad gap and not a layout choice; the gap meets the 1.5 mm clearance and falls short of the 2.0 mm pad-to-pad terminal clearance and the 2.6 mm creepage. The same footprint and circuit as the main board's R704, R705, R714 and R715, whose 1.8 mm entries the machine's record carries (open-items.md, 'A barrier the basis does not list'); accepted by the owner on 2026-11-18 as one of the 1206 gate-resistor pad pairs (owner-sign-off.md W-4, W-9 1b). The part is not changed, and the entry excuses only this pair of pads. Measured on the committed board: 1.80 mm; accepted down to 1.79 mm.; bounded by: Each pad pair is functional insulation inside one switched branch: a tracking short across it fails 'heater on' or 'gate network shorted', both bounded by the interlock relay K201 (which acts when the controller stops refreshing it or the 5 V rail collapses) and the firmware's stuck-on current threshold with every heater commanded off, which must trip at 2.0 A or less (REQ-ACTUATION-CONFIRMATION-001.C10, a requirement no node delivers yet), and by the element's own thermostats and cutoffs; none is a shock path (owner-sign-off.md W-4, W-6, W-9 1b, and the failure-mode table of circuit-proof.md). The conformal coat comes after bring-up and is no part of the bound until it is applied.

### Plan and free pass

**Plan against layout.** The floorplan holds: the six rule areas stand at the plan's bounds on both copper layers, read from the board file's zone polygons (mains (20, 20) to (65, 50.19), (20, 50.19) to (47, 66.21), (20, 66.21) to (65, 88) and (20, 88) to (55, 96); low voltage (56, 50.19) to (65, 66.21) and (55, 88) to (65, 96)) and drawn on every placement tile; the connector table holds (J941, J921 and J911 at x 24.50, 32.60 and 44.00, y 90.04; J931 pin 1 at (63.18, 58.20), pins 2 and 3 at 60.64 and 58.10); the outline is the rectangle (20.0, 21.0) to (65.0, 96.0), 45.0 by 75.0 mm, with the two 1.5 mm slots (y 49.44 to 50.94 and y 65.46 to 66.96, from x 51.0 to the right edge) open at the right edge and round-ended at x 51.0, read from the Edge.Cuts lines and arcs of the board file; H1 stands at (61.50, 92.50); all 15 footprints are on the front, none on the back; the placement command measured 0 parts outside their regions. The plan's `yes` rows and the board's own items were read against the views and the board file, each by its verdict row, and the board departs from no row that an earlier closure does not already cover. Two statements of the plan are read for the change: the baseline `MFG-10` row and decision D2 say no surface-mount part is placed by the assembler and no fiducial is carried, and since the change the assembler places six; the amendment note of 2026-11-23 in `layout-plan.md` and D2's own amendment say `MFG-10` now applies and is met by the signed exception, and the board does carry no fiducial, as D2 says; I judged `MFG-10` on its own row and the report, not on that note. Where the baseline BOARD-8 row and relaxation R3 quote 20.73 mm and 20.7 mm for U911, the board's figures are 22.41 mm body to body, 19.59 mm courtyard to body and 19.82 mm nearest pad to body, which `layout-plan.md` line 16 and its amendment note after round 9 state and item 9 of the owner's end-of-work list carries; I reproduced them from the board file (U911's F.Fab outline top at y 47.0 and its courtyard top at y 44.19 against the T911 body front at y 24.6, and its pad at y 44.425).

**What changed since round 10, read from the files.** The board and the library footprint: the eight lines under Board judged, and nothing else of the design files; no copper, silk, mask, drill or outline (the twelve exported files are identical), the views and the placement measures are Round 10's (LENGTH 373.5906 mm, CROSSINGS 7, OUTSIDE 0), and the 80 silk graphics and 25 texts, the 20 tracks, the one via and the 14 zones are as the board file held them. The method: `docs/board-design-method.md` changed in two places only, the Ordering section's export commands (the placement and parts-list steps now take the footprints fetched from the assembler, the fitting record and the unavailable-parts list) and the text of `MFG-10`, which now provides for a signed `assembler_fiducials` statement; the catalogue still holds 71 items and the board's own items 18, 89 in all, each with the kind and severity Round 10 recorded, and the 18 rows of the board's own items table in `layout-plan.md` are unchanged (the plan's three changed places are D2's amendment, the amendment note of 2026-11-23 and the `MFG-10` line of Pass 1, line 402, see finding 11.1). The tool: `hardware/tools/board_check.py` (commit `c0c3af89`) reads the optional `assembler_fiducials` mapping of the board rules record and, when it is signed, takes the `side_fiducials` lines of the board and reports each as `ACCEPTED`; an unsigned or stale one excuses nothing (`hardware/README.md`, the paragraph "The assembler fiducials statement"); the one new line of the report is the wall board's `side_fiducials` line. The records: `assembler-fitting-record.md` (new), `placement-overrides.yaml` (new: U911 at (60.64, -50.19) rotation 0 and U921 at (60.64, -66.21) rotation 180 in the position file's frame, which agree with the board positions (60.64, 50.19) and (60.64, 66.21) with y negated, the 0.165 mm residual being (9.53 - 9.20) / 2), `parts-specification.yaml` (all 14 numbered positions `fitted_by: assembler`, H1 `fitted_by: none`, which agrees with the board file, where H1 alone keeps `exclude_from_pos_files`), `board-rules.yaml` (the signed statement), `owner-sign-off.md` (W-18 and item 10 of the end-of-work list), `ordering-record.md`, `production-files-check.md`, and dated amendments in `fabrication-notes.md`, `layout-plan.md`, `layout-hedges.md`, `open-items.md`, `design-rules.md`, `circuit-proof.md` and `constraints-figures.md`; I read each amendment against the board file and found no figure that the board contradicts, apart from the matter of finding 11.1. The JLCPCB pages the statement rests on were fetched and read in this round (the capabilities table row Fiducials: Economic PCBA Not necessary, Standard PCBA Necessary; the row Assembly Types: single sided placement (SMT/Thru-hole) for Economic; FAQ item 12: "No, you don't have to. We'll add fiducial marks for SMT assembly."), and the maker's board capability page was fetched and read against the Board Setup rows, the copper-to-hole row (PTH to track 0.28 mm, 0.35 mm recommended) included.

**Every closure of an earlier round re-tested against the board as it stands.** 1.1, 1.2, 1.3, 1.4, 1.7 and 1.8: the heavy-strip copper is as rounds 3 to 10 judged it and the reasons hold, so their 2.6: no live record says 76 mm outside the WORK-9 baseline row and its amendment note (`0.76 mm` in `open-items.md` and `schematic-spec.yaml` is the maker's pad gap). 2.7 and 2.8: `owner-sign-off.md` says the board's own basis carries W-2 and W-4 and the report's `spacing_exception` lines for the wall board number 24, accepted_exceptions[0] to [23]; the one `+5V_WB` via and H1's flag are stated as the board has them. 4.1, 4.2, 5.1, 6.1, 6.2: every one of the 80 silk graphics of the board file is 0.15 mm or more (79 at 0.15 mm and one filled bolt at 0.25 mm) and the 15 reference texts are 1.0 mm at 0.15 mm thickness, every other F.SilkS text 1.0 mm at 0.15 mm or 1.5 mm at 0.2 mm. 7.1 and 7.2: from the text anchors and the F.Cu copper polygons each F.SilkS legend lies wholly on bare laminate or wholly inside one F.Cu feature (the six blade legends inside the RET and OUT_S strips, OUT_C and COFFEE, the header legend, the title block, the hazard block, the triac names and the maker lines on laminate), and the right edge of the front OUT_C zone is one straight line at x 57.95 from y 25.2 to y 46.4. 7.3 and 7.4: the live records name no round as the closing round (`owner-sign-off.md` W-13, its keeper's note and item 8 say the last round recorded). 9.1 and 9.2: the amendment note after round 9 in `layout-plan.md` carries the three measured figures and item 9 stands in the end-of-work list.

**Stale statements.** Sweeps over `hardware/sunbeam-em7000/heater-wall-board`, `docs/sunbeam-em7000`, `docs/`, `hardware/README.md` and `hardware/tools`, with `layout-review.md`, `sweep-log.md` and `schematic-review.md` read as frozen history and the board file read in this round, for the phrasings `hand-fit`, `hand fit`, `hand_fit`, `hand: true`, `fitted by hand`, `hand-solder`, `by hand`, `exclude_from_pos`, `bare-pcb`, `no assembly`, `assembly service`, `nothing is assembled`, `fiducial`, `MFG-10`, `position file`, `placement file`, `76 mm`, `closing round` and the opto-triac distances. A record that still says a surface-mount part is hand-fitted or excluded from the position file stands only where it is a dated baseline or history with the 2026-11-23 amendment beside it: the opening paragraph, the `MFG-4` and `MFG-10` rows, decision D2 and the Pass 1 line of `layout-plan.md` (the amendment note of 2026-11-23 names the first three, D2 carries its own amendment, and the Pass 1 line is finding 11.1), `layout-hedges.md` (hedge 17 and the paragraph after the table), `open-items.md` (the mechanical-symbol item, the gate-resistor item, the wide-land item and the soldering item, each amended), `constraints-figures.md`, `circuit-proof.md` (the opto-triac line and the gate-network paragraph), `owner-sign-off.md` (history of W-9 and W-11 and the option A note), `production-files-check.md` (its Preview line and its Parts note, which its own 2026-11-23 amendment lists as no longer holding), `parts-specification.yaml` (comments of history), `fabrication-notes.md` (its Parts row and its fitting-order section, amended and marked open for the triac seat height) and, in `docs/sunbeam-em7000`, the amendment banners of `controller-board-division.md`, `controller-board-cost-estimate.md` and the note in `controller-board-function-forms.md`. I found no live statement, outside those, that the board contradicts, apart from finding 11.1; the counts the records state agree with the board file: 15 footprints, 20 tracks, 1 via, 14 zones of which 6 are rule areas and 8 filled strips, 25 `gr_text` items (22 on F.SilkS, 1 on B.SilkS, 2 on F.Fab), 22 locked items (14 tracks and 8 zones), 80 silk graphics, 12 DRC clearance items, 24 `spacing_exception` lines, 15 `FIGURE` lines of the wall board, 6 assembled surface-mount parts and 14 positions in the position file (every footprint but H1).

**Silkscreen, tile by tile.** Every F.SilkS text and graphic was looked at on the three layer-F.SilkS tiles at full size, and the back on the three layer-B.SilkS tiles, which hold only the drill circles and the mirrored order-number text. Alignment: the J931 legend is right-aligned to x 55.55 on 1.5 mm rows, the board title block right-aligned to x 64.3, the blade legends read upward in pairs, the pin-1 dots at the pin-1 corners of U911 (beside pad 1 at (58.1, 54.955)) and U921 (beside pad 1 at (63.18, 61.445)), the hazard block centred on x 44.45 on bare laminate. Sizes, read from the board file: all 15 references are 1.0 mm at 0.15 mm, the other F.SilkS texts 1.5 mm at 0.2 mm or 1.0 mm at 0.15 mm. The change of 2026-11-23 moved no silk.

**Dead copper, necks, asymmetry, tested.** Every zone has one filled polygon and no island (read from the file); every track ends on a pad, a via, a zone or another track of its net (KiCad reports 0 unconnected items and no dangling track). The necks are the two triac pin-2 waists of 0.58 mm, the 0.98 mm OUT_S finger that carries only the opto-triac's gate current (my slice reads 0.73 mm across at y 70 where the finger meets the rounded end, 0.98 mm in the body), the RET back-layer tab at RV911 and the chamfers into the pads; none is new. The two chains are identical in their parts and differ in copper as the circuit requires.

**Free pass.** Seen on every view and not asked by any item or plan row: one thing wrong, a record and not the board, raised as finding 11.1: the change edited the frozen Pass 1 section of `layout-plan.md`, and the routing record's own check, `board_history.py --routed`, now fails for it, where Round 10 recorded it passing. Seen and not raised, with the reasons: (1) BOARD-8: the verdict is a pass on the body distance, 22.41 mm, as rounds 9 and 10 read it; on the baseline row's own arithmetic (courtyard top against the triac body) the figure is 19.59 mm and the nearest pad 19.82 mm, under the 20.0 mm of hedge 8, which item 9 of the owner's end-of-work list carries for his confirmation, together with the neighbour rise that W-5d carries to bring-up; the change of 2026-11-23 moves neither part; (2) the signature of the `assembler_fiducials` statement is the agent's, on the owner's behalf under his standing instruction of 2026-11-07, as W-18 and item 10 of the end-of-work list say; the check accepts it, MFG-10 is of quality severity, and the method's person-only acceptance is for a safety closure; the owner confirms it as one item; (3) the statement holds for Economic assembly only, as the record says, and the order's choice of Economic is the owner's act at the order (`ordering-record.md`); (4) the assembler's own footprint for C899868 has its pad rows 9.20 mm apart against the board's 9.53 mm, which the recorded overrides place with 0.165 mm on every pad, and the assembler's preview of the real upload is owed at the order, as the production-files record says; the opto-triacs reflow at the Economic peak of 255 +- 5 degC against the sheet's 260 degC (`open-items.md`); the triacs' seat height under assembler fitting is an open question the fabrication notes and the order remarks carry; none is a layout fact; (5) `layout-plan.md` line 3 and the baseline rows still read "hand-fitted" and are baseline text, amended by the note of 2026-11-23, as the plan's convention has it; (6) `CONN-1` and the varistors RV911 and RV921, judged not applicable for the reasons in its row, a reading of the method's own words (the varistor and the transient voltage suppressor are separate parts in SURGE-1) that places the mains surge clamps by the triacs they protect and 50 mm from the blades; (7) the tented +5V_WB via at (58.1, 56.65) under the top line of J931's silk box, as in rounds 4, 6 to 10, flagged by no check; RV911's reference reads horizontally and RV921's upward; the OUT_C legend pair stands 2.1 mm lower than the other two pairs; the B.SilkS order-number placeholder `JLCJLCJLCJLC` on the back; the 3D views draw no part models (every footprint declares none), so heights rest on the records; on F.Fab, which the maker does not build, the reference texts of RV911, RV921 and R921 read upside down and J931's fab reference overlaps its pin 2 hole. Known, accepted and not raised again: the four disclosed `hw:review-history` failures (`round structure`, `round 1 as first recorded`, `round 3 order`, `round 5 as first recorded`); the 12 DRC clearance items, all the accepted 1.04 mm pad pairs; the 0.20 mm triac annulus; the 0.58 mm waist under each triac pin 2; the mains copper 1.01 mm from the top edge (the signed plate basis); the OUT_C B.Cu-only stretch and the other relaxations on the owner's list. The record with this round appended parses with the history command's own `Round` class (`board_review_history.Round`): 0 faults, one `Round kind` line (full), a verdict table of 89 rows of 6 cells under the method's header, and a Findings part holding one table with one finding. This round raises one finding, of the routing record, so it does not close the review: finding 11.1 is closed by a change to `layout-plan.md`, which the next full round judges.
